CR 1 (XP 200; PB +2)

Traits

Rimed Hide. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 13, any creature that starts its turn in a 5-foot Emanation originating from the dire wolf. Failure: The target has the Poisoned condition until the start of its next turn. Success: The target is immune to this creature's aura for 24 hours.

Pack Tactics. The dire wolf has Advantage on an attack roll against a creature if at least one of the dire wolf's allies is within 5 feet of the creature and the ally doesn't have the Incapacitated condition.

Actions

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +5, reach 5 ft. 8 (1d10 + 3) Piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Prone condition.

Frost Shoulder Shove. Strength Saving Throw: DC 13, one creature within 5 feet. Failure: The target is pushed up to 15 feet away from the dire wolf and has the Prone condition.

CR 24 (XP 62,000; PB +7)

Traits

Amphibious. The leviathan can breathe air and water.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the leviathan fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Actions

Multiattack. The leviathan makes three Bite attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Drowning Dirge.

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +16, reach 15 ft. 36 (6d8 + 9) Piercing damage.

Drowning Dirge. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 24, each creature of the leviathan's choice in a 120-foot Emanation originating from the leviathan that can see or hear the leviathan. Failure: The target has the Charmed condition until the end of its next turn, and while Charmed this way, it must move toward the leviathan by the safest available route on its turn and can't take Reactions.

Bonus Actions

Umbral Step. While in Dim Light or Darkness, the leviathan takes the Hide action.

Legendary Actions

Legendary Action Uses: 3. Immediately after another creature's turn, the leviathan can expend a use to take one of the following actions. It regains all expended uses at the start of each of its turns.

Sink Away. The leviathan moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks, and it makes one Bite attack.

Slip Beneath. While in Dim Light or Darkness, the leviathan takes the Hide action. The leviathan can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.

Echo Terror. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 24, one creature within 30 feet. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the end of its next turn.

[concept] identity: The leviathan haunts the lightless deep in silence, then rises beneath a fleet and looses its song — a subsonic dirge that shatters hulls and drags sailors down in the echo. Between strikes it sinks back into the black water and circles unseen, hunting the vibrations of the dying above. Pressed or wounded, it does not flee; it redoubles the song into a crushing roar, drowning everything that still floats, until the survivors scatter or the leviathan falls silent.
[concept] lore: Born in the crushing dark of the trench where no sun has ever reached, the leviathan sings to feed — its song stuns, drowns, and calls the drowned down to be devoured in its lightless kingdom.

```
Oxbow Drowner
Medium beast, unaligned
Armor Class

11 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

22 (3d8 + 9)

Speed: 

30 ft., swim 30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


12 (+1)

	

8 (-1)

	

16 (+3)

	

4 (-3)

	

10 (+0)

	

4 (-3)

Skills: 

Athletics +3, Perception +2

Saving Throws: 

STR +3

Senses: 

darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 12

Languages: 

None

Challenge

1 (200 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+2

Mire Stride. Difficult terrain composed of muck, reeds, and shallow water doesn't cost the drowner extra movement, and the drowner can move across muck, reeds, and shallow water without needing to make an ability check.
Whelm. As a bonus action, the drowner whelms one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While whelmed, the target is blinded and restrained, has total cover against attacks and other effects outside the drowner, and takes 3 (1d6) bludgeoning damage at the start of each of the drowner's turns. The drowner can have only one creature whelmed at a time. If the drowner takes 5 damage or more in a single turn from the whelmed creature, the drowner must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the drowner. If the drowner dies, a whelmed creature pulls free prone by using 5 feet of movement.

Actions
Multiattack: The drowner makes two Bite attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Mudslide Haul.
Bite: Melee Weapon Attack: +3 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 5 (1d8 + 1) piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 11). While grappled, the target is restrained.
Mudslide Haul: One creature within 5 feet of the drowner that it can see must make a DC 13 Strength saving throw, taking 3 (1d6) bludgeoning damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. On a failed save, if the target is Medium or smaller, it is also knocked prone.
```

Medium Undead, Neutral

AC 11

Resistances Acid, Bludgeoning, Cold, Fire, Lightning, Piercing, Slashing, Thunder

Immunities Necrotic, Poison; Charmed, Exhaustion, Frightened, Grappled, Paralyzed, Petrified, Poisoned, Prone, Restrained

Senses darkvision 60 ft.; Passive Perception 13

Languages Common plus one other language

CR 3 (XP 700; PB +2)

Traits

Fear Aura. The kneleman emanates an aura in a 20-foot Emanation while it doesn't have the Incapacitated condition. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 13, any enemy that starts its turn in the aura. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the start of its next turn.

Ethereal Sight. The kneleman can see 60 feet into the Ethereal Plane when it is on the Material Plane.

Incorporeal Movement. The kneleman can move through other creatures and objects as if they were Difficult Terrain. It takes 5 (1d10) Force damage if it ends its turn inside an object.

Actions

Multiattack. The kneleman makes two Withering Touch attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Beckoning Pull.

Withering Touch. Melee Attack Roll: +5, reach 5 ft. 19 (3d10 + 3) Necrotic damage.

Beckoning Pull. Strength Saving Throw: DC 13, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: The target is pulled up to 15 feet straight toward the kneleman, and its Speed is 0 until the start of the kneleman's next turn.

Horrific Visage. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 13, each creature in a 60-foot Cone that can see the kneleman and isn't an Undead. Failure: 10 (2d6 + 3) Psychic damage, and the target has the Frightened condition until the start of the kneleman's next turn. Success: The target is immune to this ghost's Horrific Visage for 24 hours.

Possession (Recharge 6). Charisma Saving Throw: DC 13, one Humanoid the kneleman can see within 5 feet. Failure: The target is possessed by the kneleman; the kneleman disappears, and the target has the Incapacitated condition and loses control of its body. The kneleman now controls the body, but the target retains awareness. The kneleman can't be targeted by any attack, spell, or other effect, except ones that specifically target Undead. The kneleman's game statistics are the same, except it uses the possessed target's Speed, as well as the target's Strength, Dexterity, and Constitution modifiers.
The possession lasts until the body drops to 0 Hit Points or the kneleman leaves as a Bonus Action. When the possession ends, the kneleman appears in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the target, and the target is immune to this ghost's Possession for 24 hours. Success: The target is immune to this ghost's Possession for 24 hours.

Etherealness. The kneleman casts the Etherealness spell, requiring no spell components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability. The kneleman is visible on the Material Plane while on the Border Ethereal and vice versa, but it can't affect or be affected by anything on the other plane.

Quarry Scree Pit
Huge elemental, neutral
Armor Class

13 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

95 (10d12 + 30)

Speed: 

30 ft., burrow 30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

8 (-1)

	

16 (+3)

	

4 (-3)

	

10 (+0)

	

4 (-3)

STR +7

poison

Condition Immunities: 

exhaustion, poisoned

tremorsense 60 ft., passive Perception 13

Languages: 

None

Challenge

5 (1,800 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+3

Swallowing Scree. Difficult terrain composed of broken stone, gravel, or rubble doesn't cost the scree pit extra movement, and the scree pit can move across broken stone, gravel, or rubble without needing to make an ability check.
Entomb. As a bonus action, the scree pit entombs one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While entombed, the target is blinded and restrained, has total cover against attacks and other effects outside the scree pit, and takes 7 (2d6) bludgeoning damage at the start of each of the scree pit's turns. The scree pit can have only one creature entombed at a time. If the scree pit takes 15 damage or more in a single turn from the entombed creature, the scree pit must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the scree pit. If the scree pit dies, an entombed creature breaks free prone by using 5 feet of movement.

Actions
Multiattack: The scree pit makes two Rubble Slam attacks.
Rubble Slam: Melee Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 17 (3d8 + 4) bludgeoning damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 14). While grappled, the target is restrained.
Sliding Scree (Recharge 5-6): Each creature within 10 feet of the scree pit must make a DC 14 Dexterity saving throw, taking 36 (8d8) bludgeoning damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
```

[concept] identity: The scree-pit lies in wait beneath a thin crust of loose rock at the bottom of the quarry, and when prey crosses it the whole slope convulses, shedding a cascading wave of living stone that drags the victim down into its grinding heart. Every round after that it pulses and churns, tightening its grip of tumbling rubble and showering fresh scree over anything that tries to climb free, burying its catch by inches. It is patient and utterly without malice, a mindless landslide that only lets go when the struggling stops.
[concept] lore: Born when a quarry was abandoned mid-cut and the stone itself grew hungry, the pit-thing is the mine's memory of every rockfall that ever buried a miner, and it wants only to be fed the weight of living things.

```
Vampire
Medium undead, lawful evil
Armor Class

16 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

195 (23d8 + 92)

Speed: 

40 ft., climb 40 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

18 (+4)

	

18 (+4)

	

17 (+3)

	

15 (+2)

	

18 (+4)

Skills: 

Perception +7, Stealth +9

Saving Throws: 

DEX +9, INT +8

Damage Resistances: 

necrotic

Damage Immunities: 

poison

Condition Immunities: 

exhaustion, poisoned

Senses: 

darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 17

Languages: 

Common plus two other languages

Challenge

13 (10,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+5

Garden of Thorns. At the end of each of the vampire's turns, each creature within 5 feet of the vampire takes 13 (3d8) necrotic damage.
Legendary Resistance (3/Day, or 4/Day in Lair). If the vampire fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Misty Escape. If the vampire drops to 0 hit points outside its resting place, the vampire uses Shape-Shift to become mist (no action required). If it can't use Shape-Shift, it is destroyed.
While it has 0 hit points in mist form, it can't return to its vampire form, and it must reach its resting place within 2 hours or be destroyed. Once in its resting place, it returns to its vampire form and is paralyzed until it regains any hit points, and it regains 1 hit point after spending 1 hour there.
Spider Climb. The vampire can climb difficult surfaces, including along ceilings, without needing to make an ability check.
Vampire Weakness. The vampire has these weaknesses: Forbiddance: the vampire can't enter a residence without an invitation from an occupant.
Running Water: the vampire takes 20 acid damage if it ends its turn in running water.
Stake to the Heart: If a weapon that deals piercing damage is driven into the vampire's heart while the vampire is incapacitated in its resting place, the vampire is paralyzed until the weapon is removed.
Sunlight: the vampire takes 20 radiant damage if it starts its turn in sunlight. While in sunlight, it has disadvantage on attack rolls and ability checks.
Shape-Shift (Bonus Action). If the vampire isn't in sunlight or running water, it shape-shifts into a Tiny bat (Speed 5 ft., fly speed 30 ft.) or a Medium cloud of mist (Speed 5 ft., fly speed 20 ft. [hover]), or it returns to its vampire form. Anything it is wearing transforms with it.
While in bat form, the vampire can't speak. Its game statistics, other than its size and Speed, are unchanged.
While in mist form, the vampire can't take any actions, speak, or manipulate objects. It is weightless and can enter an enemy's space and stop there. If air can pass through a space, the mist can do so, but it can't pass through liquid. It has resistance to all damage, except the damage it takes from sunlight.
Charm (Recharge 5-6) (Bonus Action). The vampire casts charm person, requiring no spell components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17), and the duration is 24 hours. The charmed target is a willing recipient of the vampire's Bite, the damage of which doesn't end the spell. When the spell ends, the target is unaware it was charmed by the vampire.

Actions
Multiattack: The vampire makes two Grave Strike attacks and uses Bite.
Grave Strike: Melee Weapon Attack: +9 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 8 (1d8 + 4) bludgeoning damage plus 7 (2d6) necrotic damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 17) by one of two hands.
Thorned Brambles (Recharge 5-6): One creature the vampire can see within 120 feet must make a DC 17 Strength saving throw, taking 13 (3d8) necrotic damage on a failed save, and the target is restrained until the brambles is destroyed (AC 15; HP 20; vulnerability to fire damage; immunity to poison and psychic damage).
Bite (Bat or Vampire Form Only): One creature within 5 feet that is willing or that is grappled, incapacitated, or restrained must make a DC 17 Constitution saving throw, taking 6 (1d4 + 4) piercing damage plus 13 (3d8) necrotic damage on a failed save. The target's hit point maximum decreases by an amount equal to the necrotic damage taken, and the vampire regains hit points equal to that amount. A humanoid reduced to 0 hit points by this damage and then buried rises the following sunset as a vampire spawn under the vampire's control.
Legendary Actions

The vampire can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The vampire regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Deathless Strike: The vampire moves up to half its Speed, and it makes one Grave Strike attack.
Beguile: The vampire casts command, requiring no spell components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17). The vampire can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
```

Multiattack: The nave choir makes three attacks, using Hand or Choral Bolt. It can replace one attack with a use of Drowning Hymn.
Hand: Melee Spell Attack: +7 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 12 (2d8 + 3) bludgeoning damage plus 7 (2d6) necrotic damage.
Choral Bolt: Ranged Spell Attack: +7 to hit, range 80/320 ft., one target. Hit: 16 (3d8 + 3) necrotic damage.
Drowning Hymn: One creature within 60 feet of the nave choir must make a DC 15 Constitution saving throw, taking 18 (4d8) necrotic damage on a failed save. On a failed save, the target is also deafened until the end of its next turn.
Spellcasting: The nave choir casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 15):

1/day each:: charm person, blindness/deafness, hypnotic pattern, suggestion
Debt of Ruin: One creature within 60 feet of the nave choir must make a DC 15 Dexterity saving throw. On a failed save, the target is cursed until the end of the nave choir's next turn. [concept] identity: The choir opens by drawing breath as one and releasing a choral hymn that coils around the living like cold water, stripping away their actions before a single note of harm is dealt. Each round it layers a new verse — a ray of frozen hymnody, a curse that leaves a target deaf and drowning-voiceless, a command that forces a body to sway in time with the choir — so foes are puppeted, never gutted. Under pressure it does not flee; it sinks back into the flooded nave and changes its register, singing softly until the intruders stand still in the dark water and let the choir close over them.
[concept] lore: When a cathedral drowned with its congregation still singing, the choir did not stop — now it haunts the flooded nave, pulling living voices into its one endless hymn so it is never again a chorus of only the dead.

Traits

Molten Footprints. At the end of each of the tyrant's turns, each creature in a 5-foot Emanation originating from the tyrant takes 13 (3d8) Fire damage.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the tyrant fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Immutable Form. The tyrant can't shape-shift.

Siege Monster. The tyrant deals double damage to objects and structures.

Actions

Multiattack. The tyrant makes two Furnace Fist attacks.

Furnace Fist. Melee Attack Roll: +12, reach 10 ft. 20 (4d6 + 6) Bludgeoning damage plus 14 (4d6) Fire damage.

Molten Sheeting (Recharge 5-6). Constitution Saving Throw: DC 19, each creature in a 30-foot Cone. Failure: 72 (16d8) Fire damage, and the target has the Prone condition. Success: Half damage only.

Legendary Actions

Legendary Action Uses: 3. Immediately after another creature's turn, the tyrant can expend a use to take one of the following actions. It regains all expended uses at the start of each of its turns.

Crush Defiance. The tyrant moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks, and it makes one Furnace Fist attack.

Walk Through Walls. The tyrant moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks. During this movement, it can move through Large or smaller objects and structures, destroying them.

Seismic Strike. Strength Saving Throw: DC 19, each creature within 10 feet of the tyrant. Failure:

📁

Immunities Poison; Charmed, Exhaustion, Frightened, Grappled, Paralyzed, Petrified, Poisoned, Prone, Restrained, Stunned

Senses blindsight 60 ft. (blind beyond this radius); Passive Perception 10

Languages Understands the languages of its creator but can't speak

CR 13 (XP 10,000; PB +5)

Traits

Resonant Body. A creature that hits the swarm with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 10 (3d6) Thunder damage and has the Deafened condition until the end of its next turn.

Swarm. The swarm can occupy another creature's space and vice versa, and the swarm can move through any opening large enough for a Tiny creature. The swarm can't regain Hit Points or gain Temporary Hit Points.

Actions

Tolling Slam. Melee Attack Roll: +10, reach 5 ft. 54 (11d8 + 5) Bludgeoning damage plus 36 (8d8) Thunder damage, or 27 (6d8) Bludgeoning damage plus 18 (4d8) Thunder damage if the swarm is Bloodied.

Funeral Knell (Recharge 5-6). Constitution Saving Throw: DC 17, each creature in a 30-foot Emanation originating from the swarm. Failure: 67 (15d8) Thunder damage, and the target has the Deafened condition until the end of its next turn. Success: Half damage only.

[concept] identity: A single swarm-body of blackened iron funeral bells drifts silent over a battlefield until it finds a dying creature, then descends to ring itself in a rising, drowning knell that hammer-blows the air with concussive sound and drives the near-dead into final sleep. Every round it repositions as a coordinated choir, clustering around the weakest foe to peal its death-toll directly over them, and it fights to the last bell — a construct with no fear, toying with the dying only by choice of tempo. When bloodied it splits into two smaller flocks that ring in antiphonal counterpoint, harrying from opposite sides.
[concept] lore: Forged in a cathedral foundry where the guild rang a bell for every plague-dead and none for the poor, this choir of unclaimed iron bells animates only to finish the deaths the living left unmarked, hunting for the dying that no one mourned.

Reliquary Ooze
Large ooze, unaligned
Armor Class

10 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

153 (18d10 + 54)

20 ft., climb 20 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA

acid

charmed, deafened, exhaustion, frightened, grappled, prone, restrained

blindsight 60 ft., passive Perception 9

Languages: 

None

Challenge

9 (5,000 XP)

+4

Relic Adhesion. The ooze adheres to anything that touches it. A Huge or smaller creature adhered to the ooze is also grappled by it (escape DC 15). Ability checks made to escape this grapple have disadvantage.
Amorphous. The ooze can move through a space as narrow as 1 inch wide without squeezing.
Corrosive Form. A creature that touches the ooze or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 9 (2d8) acid damage.
Spider Climb. The ooze can climb difficult surfaces, including along ceilings, without needing to make an ability check.

Actions
Multiattack: The ooze makes two Pseudopod attacks.
Pseudopod: Melee Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 16 (3d8 + 3) bludgeoning damage plus 11 (2d10) acid damage.

[concept] lore: Born from spilled consecrated oils and the dissolved remains of a dozen saints, the cathedral-vault ooze has no thought but hunger for relics, and it will crawl up the altar steps until every reliquary in the diocese is soup.

Weapon Lodge. When a creature hits Kragmaw with a nonmagical weapon, the weapon lodges in Kragmaw's body. A lodged weapon can be pulled free by a creature within reach that succeeds on a DC 18 Strength (Athletics) check as an action; while lodged, Kragmaw can use it: its Stone Club attacks deal an extra 14 (4d6) bludgeoning damage per lodged weapon (maximum two).
Punishing Pursuit. As a bonus action, Kragmaw targets one creature within 30 feet. The target must succeed on a DC 18 Wisdom saving throw or have disadvantage on attack rolls against creatures other than Kragmaw until the start of Kragmaw's next turn.

Actions
Multiattack: Kragmaw makes two attacks: one with Stone Club and one with Boulder.
Stone Club: Melee Weapon Attack: +10 to hit, reach 15 ft., one target. Hit: 30 (7d6 + 6) bludgeoning damage.
Boulder: Ranged Weapon Attack: +10 to hit, range 60/240 ft., one target. Hit: 27 (6d6 + 6) bludgeoning damage.
```

```
Hydra
Huge monstrosity, unaligned
Armor Class

15 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

184 (16d12 + 80)

Speed: 

40 ft., swim 40 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


20 (+5)

	

12 (+1)

	

20 (+5)

	

2 (-4)

	

10 (+0)

	

7 (-2)

Skills: 

Perception +6

Saving Throws: 

STR +8, CON +8

Damage Resistances: 

fire

Condition Immunities: 

blinded, charmed, deafened, frightened, stunned, unconscious

Senses: 

darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 16

Languages: 

None

Challenge

8 (3,900 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+3

Cauterizing Smoke. A creature that touches the hydra or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 7 (2d6) fire damage.
Hold Breath. The hydra can hold its breath for 1 hour.
Multiple Heads. The hydra has five heads. Whenever the hydra takes 25 damage or more on a single turn, one of its heads dies. The hydra dies if all its heads are dead.
Reactive Heads. For each head the hydra has beyond one, it gets an extra reaction that can be used only for opportunity attacks.

Actions
Multiattack: The hydra makes as many Bite attacks as it has heads.
Bite: Melee Weapon Attack: +8 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 10 (1d10 + 5) piercing damage. If the target is a creature and doesn't already have a slag-sealed wound, it is subjected to the following effect. The target must make a DC 16 Constitution saving throw, or it receives a slag-sealed wound. While wounded, the target loses 7 (2d6) hit points at the start of each of its turns. The wound closes after 1 minute, after a spell restores hit points to the target, or after the target or a creature within 5 feet of it takes an action to stanch the wound, succeeding on a DC 16 Wisdom (Medicine) check.
```

CR 10 (XP 5,900; PB +4)

Traits

Weeping Saint. Strength Saving Throw: DC 17, any creature that starts its turn in a 10-foot Emanation originating from the golem. Failure: The target has the Prone condition. Success: The target is immune to this creature's aura for 24 hours.

Immutable Form. The golem can't shape-shift.

Magic Resistance. The golem has Advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.

Actions

Multiattack. The golem makes two attacks, using Slam or Force Bolt in any combination.

Slam. Melee Attack Roll: +10, reach 5 ft. 15 (2d8 + 6) Bludgeoning damage plus 9 (2d8) Force damage.

Force Bolt. Ranged Attack Roll: +9, range 120 ft. 22 (4d10) Force damage.

Shattering Advance (Recharge 5-6). The golem moves up to its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks and can move through the spaces of Large or smaller creatures. Each creature whose space it enters is targeted once by the following effect. Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 17. Failure: 35 (10d6) Bludgeoning damage, and the target has the Prone condition. Success: Half damage, and the target is pushed 5 feet straight away.

Bonus Actions

Slow (Recharge 5-6). The golem casts the Slow spell, requiring no spell components and using Constitution as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17).

[concept] identity: The Weeping Saint opens a fight by walking straight through the chapel's threshold, stone shoulders shearing through pillar and pew alike, and it never stops moving forward — its whole loop is advance, hammer, advance, treating cover as material it is entitled to remove. Every round it brings both fists down in slow, processional blows and lets tears of mineral water spill from its carved face, leaving slick penitent pools where it has stood. It fights to the last chip of itself, patient and unhurried, and its grief never softens its purpose: it will not flee, will not pause, and will not stop until whatever it was carved to condemn is dust.
[concept] lore: A grieving sculptor carved her dead patron into the chapel's column and her tears soaked the unfinished stone, and the saint woke still carrying a sorrow it can only express by breaking whatever draws near.

```
Sordes Archon

Large Celestial, Lawful Evil

AC 18
Initiative +5 (15)

HP 190 (20d10 + 80)

Speed 40 ft., Fly 80 ft.

MOD
SAVE
STR

18

12
+1
+1

Skills Perception +8, Stealth +10

Resistances Radiant

Immunities Charmed

Senses darkvision 120 ft.; Passive Perception 18

Languages understands Celestial and Common but can't speak

CR 13 (XP 10,000; PB +5)

Traits

Swarm-Fragments. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 17, any creature that starts its turn in a 20-foot Emanation originating from the archon. Failure: The target has the Poisoned condition until the start of its next turn. Success: The target is immune to this creature's aura for 24 hours.

Wing Drift. The archon doesn't provoke Opportunity Attacks when it flies out of an enemy's reach.

Wing Sway. When a creature the archon can see ends its turn within 5 feet of it, the archon can move up to 10 feet without provoking Opportunity Attacks.

Actions

Multiattack. The archon makes three Talons attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Swarm of Flies.

Talons. Melee Attack Roll: +10, reach 10 ft. 14 (2d8 + 5) Slashing damage plus 10 (3d6) Poison damage. If the archon flew at least 20 feet in a straight line toward the target immediately before the hit, the target takes an extra 7 (2d6) Slashing damage and, if it is Large or smaller, has the Prone condition.

Descending Veil (Recharge 5-6). The archon flies up to its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks. Each creature it passes within 5 feet during this movement is targeted once by the following effect. Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 17. Failure: 67 (15d8) Poison damage, and the target has the Prone condition. Success: Half damage.

Swarm of Flies. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 17, each creature in a 30-foot Cone. Failure: The target has the Blinded condition until the start of the archon's next turn.

Bonus Actions

Aerial Agility. The archon takes the Dash or Disengage action.

[concept] identity: The Plaguebearing Archon opens by unfolding a curtain of ragged wings and loosing its lesser swarm-fragments into the air, rolling a veil of biting flies across the field before it dives. Round after round it skirmishes on the wing — swooping to rake prey with filth-crusted talons, snatching a body aloft, and dropping it, while its commanded swarms keep the wounded blinded and sickened. It is a proud, contemptuous celestial that toils over its prey and only withdraws when truly bloodied, breaking into a scattered swarm and reforming to strike again rather than flee outright.
[concept] lore: A plague-bearing archon of flies is a celestial sentinel whose judgment was turned to pestilence: it descended to cleanse a city of corruption and became the corruption, commanding its swarm as a shepherd commands a flock.

Actions

Tail Stinger. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 16 (2d6 + 9) Piercing damage plus 35 (10d6) Poison damage.

Swallowing Maw. Strength Saving Throw: DC 21, one Huge or smaller creature within 60 feet. Failure: The target is pulled up to 30 feet straight toward the worm by its grip and has the Grappled condition (escape DC 22). While Grappled this way, the target has the Restrained condition.

Adult Red Dragon
Huge dragon, chaotic evil
Armor Class

19 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

256 (19d12 + 133)

Speed: 

40 ft., fly 80 ft., climb 40 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


27 (+8)

	

10 (+0)

	

25 (+7)

	

16 (+3)

	

13 (+1)

	

23 (+6)

Skills: 

Perception +13, Stealth +6

Saving Throws: 

DEX +6, WIS +7

Damage Immunities: 

fire

Senses: 

blindsight 60 ft., darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 23

Languages: 

Common, Draconic

Challenge

17 (18,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+6

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the red dragon fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Actions
Multiattack: The red dragon makes three Rend attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Spellcasting to cast Scorching Ray.
Rend: Melee Weapon Attack: +14 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 13 (1d10 + 8) slashing damage plus 5 (2d4) fire damage.
Spellcasting: The red dragon casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 20, +12 to hit with spell attacks): -

At will: command, detect magic, scorching ray

1/day each: fireball
Fire Breath (Recharge 5-6): Each creature in a 60-foot cone must make a DC 21 Dexterity saving throw, taking 59 (17d6) fire damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
Legendary Actions

The red dragon can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The red dragon regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Commanding Presence: The red dragon uses Spellcasting to cast command (cast at 2nd level). The red dragon can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Fiery Rays: The red dragon uses Spellcasting to cast scorching ray. The red dragon can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Pounce: The red dragon moves up to half its speed, and it makes one Rend attack.
```

[concept] identity: An Adult Red Dragon whose ruined eye is a lance-wound scar, it opens a fight with a searing cone of flame down the side of its good eye, forcing prey to run through its blind flank and eat a claw or bite as they try to circle. Every round it pivots to present its intact eye, punishing flankers with a sweeping tail and a snap of its jaws, and if its remaining sight is blinded or its breath spent it wrecks the field with wing-buffets and a full-body slam rather than take flight. Scarred and prideful, it does not flee when bloodied — it hunts by sound and heat-scent, then pins the offending prey and burns it to ash.
[concept] lore: Old Aurnethrax lost his eye to a knight's lance and kept the broken tip under his hide as a lesson; now he hunts any creature that dares approach from his dead side, hoarding the lances of every challenger who thought one blinded eye meant a weak dragon.

Pit Fiend

Large Fiend, Lawful Evil

AC 21
Initiative +2 (12)

HP 337 (27d10 + 189)

Speed 30 ft., Fly 60 ft.

MOD
SAVE

MOD

+13

24
+7
+7

Skills Perception +10, Persuasion +19

Resistances Cold

Immunities Fire, Poison; Poisoned

Senses darkvision 120 ft.; Passive Perception 20

Languages Infernal; telepathy 120 ft.

CR 20 (XP 25,000; PB +6)

Traits

Broken Fealty. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 21, any creature that starts its turn in a 20-foot Emanation originating from the pit fiend. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the start of its next turn. Success: The target is immune to this creature's aura for 24 hours.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the pit fiend fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Diabolical Restoration. If the pit fiend dies outside the Nine Hells, its body disappears in sulfurous smoke, and it gains a new body instantly, reviving with all its Hit Points somewhere in the Nine Hells.

Fear Aura. The pit fiend emanates an aura in a 20-foot Emanation while it doesn't have the Incapacitated condition. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 21, any enemy that starts its turn in the aura. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the start of its next turn. Success: The target is immune to this pit fiend's aura for 24 hours.

Magic Resistance. The pit fiend has Advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.

Actions

Multiattack. The pit fiend makes one Bite attack, two Devilish Claw attacks, and one Fiery Mace attack.

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 18 (3d6 + 8) Piercing damage. If the target is a creature, it must make the following saving throw. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 21. Failure: The target has the Poisoned condition. While Poisoned, the target can't regain Hit Points and takes 21 (6d6) Poison damage at the start of each of its turns, and it repeats the save at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success. After 1 minute, it succeeds automatically.

Fiery Mace. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 22 (4d6 + 8) Force damage plus 21 (6d6) Fire damage.

Devilish Claw. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 26 (4d8 + 8) Necrotic damage.

Hellfire Spellcasting (Recharge 4-6). The pit fiend casts Fireball (level 5 version) twice, requiring no Material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 21). It can replace one Fireball with Hold Monster (level 7 version) or Wall of Fire.

Bonus Actions

Dying Oath. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 21, one creature within 30 feet. Failure: Until the start of the pit fiend's next turn, the target has Disadvantage on attack rolls against creatures other than the pit fiend.

## Sarcophagus Star-Lord — in the run

**Concept:** an archlich enthroned on a sun of bone

| block | CR | donor | derivation | bands | structure | gates | headerBody | edition | donor | adjudicability | reader | class |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Sarcophagus Star-Lord | 20 (2014) | caster-21 | ok | ok | ok | ok | ok | FAIL: conversion-dropped-duration | ok | ok | ok | ok |

- edition · fail · conversion-dropped-duration: Actions/Paralyzing Touch: the 2024 row bounds Paralyzed; the 2014 sheet dropped the duration and imposes it indefinitely
- read: def 20 / off 20 → 20; sustained 126 (3 × Eldritch Burst + legendary Deathly Teleport); nova none; dmg 126

```
Sarcophagus Star-Lord
Medium undead, neutral evil
Armor Class

20 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

315 (42d8 + 126)

Speed: 

30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


11 (+0)

	

16 (+3)

	

16 (+3)

	

21 (+5)

	

14 (+2)

	

16 (+3)

Skills: 

Arcana +17, History +11, Insight +8, Perception +8

Saving Throws: 

CON +9, INT +11, WIS +8

Damage Resistances: 

cold, lightning

Damage Immunities: 

necrotic, poison

Condition Immunities: 

charmed, exhaustion, frightened, paralyzed, poisoned

Senses: 

darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 18

Languages: 

All

Challenge

20 (25,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+6

Bone Sun Throne. The star-lord keeps the bone sun within 30 feet of itself, a Tiny object that has AC 17, 25 hit points, and resistance to all damage. While it is intact, the star-lord regains 20 hit points at the start of each of its turns.
Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the star-lord fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Spirit Jar. If destroyed, the star-lord reforms in 1d10 days if it has a spirit jar, reviving with all its hit points. The new body appears in an unoccupied space within the star-lord's lair.
Protective Magic (3/Day) (Reaction). When the star-lord is hit by an attack roll, the star-lord casts shield in response, gaining a +5 bonus to AC until the start of its next turn, including against the triggering attack.

Actions
Multiattack: The star-lord makes three attacks, using Eldritch Burst or Paralyzing Touch. It can replace one attack with a use of Spellcasting to cast Bestow Curse.
Eldritch Burst: Melee or Ranged Spell Attack: +11 to hit, reach 5 ft. or range 120 ft., one target. Hit: 31 (4d12 + 5) force damage.
Paralyzing Touch: Melee Spell Attack: +11 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 15 (3d6 + 5) cold damage, and the target must succeed on a DC 19 Constitution saving throw or be paralyzed.
Spellcasting: The star-lord casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Intelligence as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 19): -

At will: detect magic, detect thoughts, dispel magic, fireball, invisibility, lightning bolt, mage hand, prestidigitation

2/day each: animate dead, dimension door, plane shift

1/day each: chain lightning, finger of death, power word kill, scrying
Legendary Actions

The star-lord can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The star-lord regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Deathly Teleport: The star-lord teleports up to 60 feet to an unoccupied space it can see, and each creature within 10 feet of the space it left takes 11 (2d10) necrotic damage.
Disrupt Life: Each creature that isn't an undead within 20 feet of the star-lord must make a DC 19 Constitution saving throw, taking 31 (9d6) necrotic damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. The star-lord can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Frightening Gaze: The star-lord casts fear, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting. The star-lord can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
```

Ancient Gold Dragon
Gargantuan dragon, lawful good
Armor Class

22 (Natural Armor)

Hit Points: 

546 (28d20 + 252)

Speed: 

40 ft., fly 80 ft., swim 40 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


30 (+10)

	

14 (+2)

	

29 (+9)

	

18 (+4)

	

17 (+3)

	

28 (+9)

Skills: 

Insight +10, Perception +17, Persuasion +16, Stealth +9

Saving Throws: 

DEX +9, CON +16, WIS +10, CHA +16

Damage Immunities: 

Fire

Senses: 

Blindsight 60 ft., Darkvision 120 ft., Passive Perception 27

Languages: 

Common, Draconic

Challenge

24

Proficiency Bonus:

+7

Amphibious. The dragon can breathe air and water.
Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the dragon fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Actions
Multiattack: The dragon can use its Frightful Presence. It then makes three attacks: one with its bite and two with its claws.
Bite: Melee Weapon Attack: +17 to hit, reach 15 ft., one target. Hit: 21 (2d10 + 10) piercing damage.
Claw: Melee Weapon Attack: +17 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 17 (2d6 + 10) slashing damage.
Tail: Melee Weapon Attack: +17 to hit, reach 20 ft., one target. Hit: 19 (2d8 + 10) bludgeoning damage.
Frightful Presence: Each creature of the dragon's choice that is within 120 feet of the dragon and aware of it must succeed on a DC 24 Wisdom saving throw or become frightened for 1 minute. A creature can repeat the saving throw at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success. If a creature's saving throw is successful or the effect ends for it, the creature is immune to the dragon's Frightful Presence for the next 24 hours.
Breath Weapons (Recharge 5–6): The dragon uses one of the following breath weapons.
Fire Breath: The dragon exhales fire in a 90-foot cone. Each creature in that area must make a DC 24 Dexterity saving throw, taking 71 (13d10) fire damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
Weakening Breath: The dragon exhales gas in a 90-foot cone. Each creature in that area must succeed on a DC 24 Strength saving throw or have disadvantage on Strength-based attack rolls, Strength checks, and Strength saving throws for 1 minute. A creature can repeat the saving throw at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success.
Change Shape: The dragon magically polymorphs into a humanoid or beast that has a challenge rating no higher than its own, or back into its true form. It reverts to its true form if it dies. Any equipment it is wearing or carrying is absorbed or borne by the new form (the dragon's choice).

In a new form, the dragon retains its alignment, hit points, Hit Dice, ability to speak, proficiencies, Legendary Resistance, lair actions, and Intelligence, Wisdom, and Charisma scores, as well as this action. Its statistics and capabilities are otherwise replaced by those of the new form, except any class features or legendary actions of that form.
Legendary Actions

The ancient gold dragon can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The ancient gold dragon regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Detect: The dragon makes a Wisdom (Perception) check.
Tail Attack: The dragon makes a tail attack.
Wing Attack (Costs 2 Actions): The dragon beats its wings. Each creature within 15 feet of the dragon must succeed on a DC 25 Dexterity saving throw or take 17 (2d6 + 10) bludgeoning damage and be knocked prone. The dragon can then fly up to half its flying speed.
```

Each round it fights like a living forge — sweeping wings, gilded talons, and a tail like a falling ingot — wearing down heroes while it judges their worth, and it never flees: a wounded gold dragon only burns hotter, toying with worthy foes and annihilating the rest. When pressed it does not retreat; it rises, circles, and descends with everything it has, because to a creature this old the fight is a test of whether the world deserves its protection.

## Moltwright Horror — in the run

**Concept:** A Large insectile horror. It has four scythe-arms, a spitting maw, and a shell that sheds acid. It fights by pinning prey with two arms and carving with the others.

Moltwright Horror

Large Monstrosity, Neutral

AC 15
Initiative -1 (9)

Skills Athletics +8, Perception +5

Senses darkvision 60 ft.; Passive Perception 15

Languages None

CR 9 (XP 5,000; PB +4)

Traits

Two-Arm Pin. While the horror has a creature Grappled, the horror keeps two of its scythe-arms locked around the Grappled creature; while it does, that creature has the Grappled condition and the horror cannot use those arms to make Spitting Maw attacks.

Caustic Shedding. At the end of each of the horror's turns, each creature in a 5-foot Emanation originating from the horror takes 7 (2d6) Acid damage.

Actions

Multiattack. The horror makes two attacks, using Spitting Maw or Four Scythe-Arms in any combination.

Spitting Maw. Melee Attack Roll: +8, reach 10 ft. 31 (6d8 + 4) Piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 16). If the horror moved at least 20 feet straight toward the target immediately before the hit and the target is Large or smaller, the target has the Prone condition, and the horror can make one Spitting Maw attack against it as a Bonus Action.

Four Scythe-Arms. Melee Attack Roll: +8, reach 5 ft. 31 (6d8 + 4) Slashing damage.

Bonus Actions

Cocoon. The horror cocoons one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While cocooned, the target has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the horror, and takes 10 (3d6) Acid damage at the start of each of the horror's turns. The horror can have only one creature cocooned at a time. If the horror takes 25 damage or more on a single turn from the cocooned creature, the horror must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits Prone in an unoccupied space within 10 feet of the horror. If the horror dies, a cocooned creature tears free Prone by using 10 feet of movement.

Huge construct, lawful neutral
Armor Class

20 (natural armor)

171 (18d12 + 54)

+4

Ceiling Crawl. The warden can climb difficult surfaces, including along ceilings, without needing to make an ability check.
Acid Absorption. Whenever the warden is subjected to acid damage, it takes no damage and instead regains a number of hit points equal to the acid damage dealt.
Immutable Form. The warden can't shape-shift.
Siege Monster. The warden deals double damage to objects and structures.

Actions
Multiattack: The warden makes two Slam attacks.
Slam: Melee Weapon Attack: +8 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 25 (6d6 + 4) bludgeoning damage.
Iron Sweep (Recharge 5-6): Each creature in a 30-foot cone must make a DC 16 Constitution saving throw, taking 66 (12d10) bludgeoning damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. On a failed save, the target is also knocked prone.

[concept] identity: A slab of black iron the size of a gatehouse, the sentinel grinds forward without haste or pause, its footfalls buckling the floor before it ever swings — the first round is a slow, awful approach that gives prey time to run and nowhere to go. Every round after, it hammers in wide sweeping blows and simply walks through anything in its path, shrugging off blows that would fell a war-band. It has no temper to speak of: it does not flee, does not toy, and does not stop until the charge it was forged to keep is dust.
[concept] lore: The black-iron sentinels were poured in one heat at a foundry long since cold, and each one still stands its post outside a gate or vault that no longer exists, waiting for an order that will never come.

**Concept:** a marsh witch who curses trespassers, cr 7

Medium Humanoid, Neutral

AC 15
Initiative +2 (12)

HP 81 (18d8)

Speed 30 ft., Swim 30 ft.

MOD

+3

Skills Arcana +6, History +6, Perception +4

Senses Passive Perception 14

Languages Common and any three languages

CR 7 (XP 2,900; PB +3)

Traits

Trespasser's Hex. The hexwife emanates an aura in a 20-foot Emanation while it doesn't have the Incapacitated condition. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 14, any enemy that starts its turn in the aura. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the start of its next turn.

Actions

Multiattack. The hexwife makes three Arcane Burst attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Bog Hex.

Arcane Burst. Melee or Ranged Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. or range 120 ft. 16 (3d8 + 3) Force damage.

Spellcasting. The hexwife casts one of the following spells, using Intelligence as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 14):

- At Will: Detect Magic, Light, Mage Armor, Mage Hand, Prestidigitation
- 2/Day Each: Fireball, Invisibility
- 1/Day Each: Cone of Cold, Fly

Bog Hex. Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 14, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: Until the end of the hexwife's next turn, the target sheds dim light in a 10-foot radius, it can't benefit from the Invisible condition, and attack rolls against it have Advantage.

Bonus Actions

Misty Step (3/Day). The hexwife casts Misty Step, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting.

Reactions

Protective Magic (3/Day). The hexwife casts Counterspell or Shield in response to the spell's trigger, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting.

[concept] identity: The marsh witch opens by cursing the intruder from cover — a shouted hex that fouls their footing and dulls their senses — then works a running retreat through the reeds, laying one condition after another while never letting anyone close enough for a fair exchange. She fights to control the ground and the tempo, not to spill blood, and slips away into the bog when a fight turns truly bad rather than trading blows to the death.
[concept] lore: Born of a drowned covenant made in the marsh's brine, she counts every trespasser a debt owed to the bog and wrings the debt out of them one curse at a time.

Multiattack. Corrin makes three Mace attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Shield Bash.

Shield Bash. Strength Saving Throw: DC 15, one creature within 5 feet that Corrin can see. Failure: 9 (2d4 + 4) Bludgeoning damage. If the target is a Medium or smaller creature, it has the Prone condition.

Ilvareth Dusksong
Medium humanoid, neutral
Armor Class

17

Hit Points: 

170 (31d8 + 31)

Speed: 

30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


10 (+0)

	

14 (+2)

	

12 (+1)

	

20 (+5)

	

15 (+2)

	

16 (+3)

Skills: 

Arcana +13, History +9, Perception +6

Saving Throws: 

INT +9, WIS +6

Damage Immunities: 

psychic, charmed ((with mind blank))

Senses: 

passive Perception 16

Languages: 

Common plus five other languages, Elvish, Undercommon

Challenge

12 (8,400 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+4

Shadow Step. Ilvareth is bound to the shadow realm. It can't willingly move more than 120 feet from the shadow realm, and it always knows the direction and distance to the shadow realm.
Magic Resistance. Ilvareth has advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.
Misty Step (3/Day). As a bonus action, Ilvareth casts misty step, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting.
Protective Magic (3/Day) (Reaction). When Ilvareth is hit by an attack roll, Ilvareth casts shield in response, gaining a +5 bonus to AC until the start of its next turn, including against the triggering attack.

Actions
Multiattack: Ilvareth makes four Arcane Burst attacks.
Arcane Burst: Melee or Ranged Spell Attack: +9 to hit, reach 5 ft. or range 150 ft., one target. Hit: 27 (4d10 + 5) force damage. Until the end of Ilvareth's next turn, Ilvareth has advantage on attack rolls against the target, and the target can't take the Hide action.
Spellcasting: Ilvareth casts one of the following spells, using Intelligence as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17):

At will:: detect magic, detect thoughts, disguise self, invisibility, light, mage armor, mage hand, prestidigitation
2/day each:: fly, lightning bolt
1/day each:: cone of cold, mind blank, scrying, teleport

Large Dragon, Chaotic Neutral

Languages Common, Draconic

CR 7 (XP 2,900; PB +3)

Traits

Evasion. If the drake is subjected to an effect that allows it to make a Dexterity saving throw to take only half damage, the drake instead takes no damage if it succeeds on the save and only half damage if it fails. It can't use this trait if it has the Incapacitated condition.

Actions

Multiattack. The drake makes two Bite attacks.

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +7, reach 5 ft. 17 (3d8 + 4) Piercing damage. If the target is a creature and doesn't already have a thunder-Deafened wound, it is subjected to the following effect. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 15. Failure: The target receives a thunder-Deafened wound. While wounded, the target loses 7 (2d6) Hit Points at the start of each of its turns. The wound closes after 1 minute, after a spell restores Hit Points to the target, or after the target or a creature within 5 feet of it takes an action to stanch the wound, succeeding on a DC 15 Wisdom (Medicine) check.

Thunderwall (Recharge 5-6). Constitution Saving Throw: DC 15, each creature in a 15-foot Cone. Failure: 42 (12d6) Thunder damage. Success: Half damage.

Mirelung Ascetic
Medium humanoid, neutral
Armor Class

15

Hit Points: 

94 (21d8)

Speed: 

30 ft., climb 30 ft., swim 30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


10 (+0)

	

16 (+3)

	

10 (+0)

	

12 (+1)

	

14 (+2)

	

16 (+3)

Skills: 

Perception +5, Stealth +6

Saving Throws: 

DEX +6

Senses: 

passive Perception 15

Languages: 

Common plus one other language, Draconic

Challenge

5 (1,800 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+3

Tail Balance. The ascetic can climb difficult surfaces, including along ceilings, without needing to make an ability check.
Hold Breath. The ascetic can hold its breath for 15 minutes.
Marsh Stride. Difficult terrain composed of mud doesn't cost the ascetic extra movement, and the ascetic can move across mud without needing to make an ability check.
Patient Defense. As a bonus action, the ascetic takes the Dodge action.
Cunning Action. As a bonus action, the ascetic takes the Dash, Disengage, or Hide action.

Actions
Multiattack: The ascetic makes two attacks: one with its Unarmed Strike and one with its Dart.
Unarmed Strike: Melee Weapon Attack: +6 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 23 (3d8 + 3) bludgeoning damage. Once on each of the ascetic's turns when it hits with this attack, the target is subjected to the following effect. The target must make a DC 14 Constitution saving throw. On a failed save, the target is stunned until the end of the ascetic's next turn. If the ascetic moved at least 20 feet straight toward the target immediately before the hit and the target is Large or smaller, the target is also knocked prone, and the ascetic can make one Knuckle attack against it as a bonus action.
Dart: Ranged Weapon Attack: +6 to hit, range 20/60 ft., one target. Hit: 12 (2d8 + 3) piercing damage.
```

Well-Drowned Thing

Large Aberration, Neutral Evil

AC 14

Skills Athletics +6, Perception +3

Senses darkvision 60 ft.; Passive Perception 13

Languages None

CR 5 (XP 1,800; PB +3)

Traits

Mire Stride. Difficult Terrain composed of water and muddy ground doesn't cost the well-drowned thing extra movement, and the well-drowned thing can move across water and muddy ground without needing to make an ability check.

Actions

Multiattack. The well-drowned thing makes two Tendril attacks.

Tendril. Melee Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. 17 (4d6 + 3) Necrotic damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 14). While Grappled, the target has the Restrained condition.

Bonus Actions

Whelm. The well-drowned thing whelms one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While whelmed, the target has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the well-drowned thing, and takes 7 (2d6) Necrotic damage at the start of each of the well-drowned thing's turns. The well-drowned thing can have only one creature whelmed at a time. If the well-drowned thing takes 15 damage or more in a single turn from the whelmed creature, the well-drowned thing must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits Prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the well-drowned thing. If the well-drowned thing dies, a whelmed creature pulls free Prone by using 5 feet of movement.

Rimeward Lattice

Tiny Construct, Lawful Good

AC 14
Initiative -1 (9)

Immunities Cold, Poison; Charmed, Exhaustion, Frightened, Paralyzed, Petrified, Poisoned

Senses darkvision 60 ft.; Passive Perception 12

Languages understands Common but can't speak

CR 4 (XP 1,100; PB +2)

Traits

Winter's Vigil. While the lattice is subjected to an effect that would force it to move or flee, the lattice can't be moved against its will and ignores effects that would compel it to leave its ground until the frost claims the field.

Frost Aura. At the end of each of the lattice's turns, each creature in a 5-foot Emanation originating from the lattice takes 5 (1d10) Cold damage.

Cold Absorption. Whenever the lattice is subjected to Cold damage, it takes no damage and instead regains a number of Hit Points equal to the Cold damage dealt.

Immutable Form. The lattice can't shape-shift.

Magic Resistance. The lattice has Advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.

Actions

Multiattack. The lattice makes two Lands Bite attacks.

Lands Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. 13 (2d8 + 4) Cold damage. If the lattice moved at least 20 feet straight toward the target immediately before the hit and the target is Large or smaller, the target has the Prone condition, and the lattice can make one Lands Bite attack against it as a Bonus Action.

[concept] synthesis text: The Frostbound Warden is a towering, thin-limbed figure of dark iron latticework that moves as a single will, though its body is a slow collective of hand-sized iron thorns mantled in frost-moss — each thorn a tiny sapient node chilled by the Astral cold it was forged to carry. It opens by raising one long arm so its thorn-flock spills forward, and every shard that lands bites with the deep cold stored in its seams, sapping warmth from limbs and nerves. It half-buries intruders in frost-hardened moss until they stand still, then simply waits, and it will not flee: it holds its ground until the frost claims the field, warned off only by a calm and respectful greeting. It is fully sapient (INT 10-14), and it is a benevolent warden sworn to protect travelers who show respect.

Large humanoid, neutral
Armor Class

16 (breastplate)

Hit Points: 

120 (14d10 + 42)

Speed: 

30 ft., swim 30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

15 (+2)

	

16 (+3)

	

10 (+0)

	

12 (+1)

	

15 (+2)

Skills: 

Perception +4, Stealth +5

Saving Throws: 

DEX +5, WIS +4

Senses: 

passive Perception 14

Languages: 

Common, Draconic

Challenge

8 (3,900 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+3

Wounded Frenzy. While Meshrin has half of its hit points or fewer, it has advantage on attack rolls and saving throws.
Hold Breath. Meshrin can hold its breath for 15 minutes.
Great Weapon Master. When Meshrin hits a creature with its Greataxe, the attack deals an extra 3 damage.
Marsh Stride. Difficult terrain composed of mud doesn't cost Meshrin extra movement, and Meshrin can move across mud without needing to make an ability check.

Actions
Multiattack: Meshrin makes three attacks, using Greataxe or Javelin.
Greataxe: Melee Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 17 (3d8 + 4) slashing damage.
Javelin: Ranged Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, range 30/120 ft., one target. Hit: 17 (3d8 + 4) piercing damage. Immediately after the attack, a javelin returns to Meshrin's grasp, and the target's speed is reduced by 10 feet until the start of Meshrin's next turn.
Thrust Roar (1/Day): One creature Meshrin can see within 60 feet that has half of its hit points or fewer must make a DC 15 Dexterity saving throw. On a failed save, if the target has 14 hit points or fewer, it drops to 0 hit points; otherwise, it takes 14 (4d6) piercing damage.
```

[concept] identity: The greatsword skeleton is a relentless line-breaker: it strides straight at the biggest target and opens with a wide overhead cleave meant to drop a shield wall in one swing. Every round after, it plants itself and trades blow for blow, wading through the front rank with patient, mechanical arcs while its bones absorb hits that would fell a living soldier. It has no fear and no scheme — it fights until the last of its frame is shattered, never yielding ground it has taken.
[concept] lore: Raised from a forgotten battlefield ossuary by a necromancer long gone, it still carries the two-handed blade it died gripping and stands its old watch, cutting down anything that crosses the ground it was buried to hold.

Corran Vantiss
Medium humanoid, neutral
Armor Class

16 (studded leather, shield)

Hit Points: 

112 (15d8 + 45)

Speed: 

30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

15 (+2)

	

16 (+3)

	

10 (+0)

	

12 (+1)

	

15 (+2)

passive Perception 11

Common

Challenge

5 (1,800 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+3

Returning Handaxes. While Corran is moving toward a creature it has damaged within the last 24 hours, Corran's speed can't be reduced and difficult terrain doesn't cost it extra movement.
Riposte (Reaction). When a creature within 5 feet of Corran misses it with a melee attack, Corran makes one Handaxe attack against that creature.

Actions
Multiattack: Corran makes three Handaxe attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Shortsword.
Handaxe: Melee or Ranged Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft. or range 20/60 ft., one target. Hit: 11 (2d6 + 4) slashing damage, and Corran has advantage on its next attack roll against the target before the end of its next turn.
Shortsword: Melee Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 8 (1d8 + 4) piercing damage. Immediately after the attack, a handaxe returns to Corran's grasp, and Corran has advantage on its next attack roll against the target before the end of its next turn.
Shield Bash: One creature within 5 feet of Corran that it can see must make a DC 15 Strength saving throw, taking 9 (2d4 + 4) bludgeoning damage on a failed save. If the target is a Medium or smaller creature, it is knocked prone.
```

[concept] identity: Brother Halric opens by wading into the thick of melee, hammer sweeping in wide radiant arcs while he bellows a war-prayer that heartens his allies and cows the faithless. Every round after that he presses the attack without pause, trading blow for blow and calling down searing light between swings; his strikes carry the same holy force as his sermons. Under pressure he grows louder and more zealous rather than cautious — he never breaks, never yields ground, and fights to the death as a martyr of his own creed.
[concept] lore: Halric was a battlefield chaplain whose congregation died around him, and he now wanders the roads of the faithful with a warhammer in one hand and a prayer-book in the other, converting the unwilling at his hammer's edge.
